## Metadata

- Full Title: This Week in Marketing: Google does Apple, OpenAI does ads, publishers do Google — What?
- Category: #articles
- Summary: OpenAI is starting ads for ChatGPT while Google faces lawsuits and changes its AI strategy. Social media and streaming platforms are evolving with personalized content and new features. Publishers worry as Google’s traffic and referrals drop, pushing shifts in advertising and AI use.

##### What was that? OpenAI does ads, Google does Apple’s Siri, multiple publishers sue Google, Instagram globalises personalised Reels algorithm, and the rest is chaos as usual.

It’s Monday? Sorry for the delay, hit peak fever last night, better now. That’s life. Thanks for your love and patience…

This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/dee5825b-91a5-4ce0-91ee-af8a34dc318c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard. Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.

* Digg and Reddit creators [relaunch](https://substack.com/redirect/fc3b0ebb-aac0-41d6-96d1-9e1da0366d73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Digg to compete with [Reddit](https://substack.com/redirect/2ad207a9-3b45-4cfd-ba85-040e8d72e80b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [Instagram](https://substack.com/redirect/bc9ffb18-16d2-4ef8-a39f-6c2f444d5cec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) wants you to [personalize](https://substack.com/redirect/97e56e7d-06ab-4710-aceb-e8422dd3d79f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) your Reels algorithm for 2026.
* [Pinterest](https://substack.com/redirect/18317982-878b-4ea9-98bd-f9a3bb705434?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) just released its third annual color forecast, [identifying](https://substack.com/redirect/fe7108de-c571-4350-9ac1-e3fc1f2391ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) five shades—Perismmon, Cool Blue, Plum Noir, and Wasabi.
* [Apple](https://substack.com/redirect/38a4a96c-257d-4876-ad1a-426ed8583bf0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to replace ChatGPT with [Google Gemini](https://substack.com/redirect/89a5a2c1-1fb5-44c9-9e0f-fc4ab9b51bc4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in $1 billion annual deal for [AI](https://substack.com/redirect/740ebb64-1e88-441f-be55-2d62ceacb5ba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* X [still allowing](https://substack.com/redirect/4149bc9c-5b05-456c-a3b7-fe05e7b5fe80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) users [to post](https://substack.com/redirect/98ae1950-0d9a-4b72-82cb-0f4f6da48f5c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) sexualised images [generated](https://substack.com/redirect/e4699eba-c326-45b4-8a24-7f88987210a7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by [Grok AI tool](https://substack.com/redirect/25e977fd-b0b8-439b-866e-8240bd4c0e19?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

* [Spotify](https://substack.com/redirect/f00094a0-28b7-4c3c-be9f-b560f17f5682?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to raise monthly subscription price to [$12.99](https://substack.com/redirect/3dd4eabd-2de8-4634-8faf-a7611f16468a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in US and other markets.
	+ Spotify’s [new CEOs](https://substack.com/redirect/53512c7c-943f-4d31-91ab-52fea8303af2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are battling algo fatigue and angry artists.
* Reddit mod demands to be treated as ‘[celebrity](https://substack.com/redirect/6501ee4e-4a89-429d-8bb6-66f7f3a117c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ after getting removed from LSF.
* [Claude](https://substack.com/redirect/689c7ece-de74-48b3-b671-f99a0d7a423f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is taking the AI world by storm, and even non-nerds are blown away.
* Inside the algo-reform: How [Jack Anderton](https://substack.com/redirect/fffca251-aaa6-408e-8da7-691a4af52476?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) became Nigel Farage’s Gen Z whisperer…
* FOX [reportedly didn’t sign off](https://substack.com/redirect/2df59274-f29a-46bc-8414-6fefd2f4751f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on FIFA selling live World Cup ‘[look-ins](https://substack.com/redirect/082c128d-6749-4829-b7b6-15d328b7b67d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ to TikTok.
* [Elon Musk](https://substack.com/redirect/911aa3c0-7b86-4693-a605-3cc4ba6e2ea7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) seeks up to $134 billion damages [from](https://substack.com/redirect/39cd9ab9-c715-483d-ac64-0b379d528fe1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) OpenAI, Microsoft.
* [The Atlantic](https://substack.com/redirect/28a2562e-6307-445e-bc4c-b64b5d99f690?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), Penske Media, Vox [sue Google](https://substack.com/redirect/120fb71d-6271-4a00-b861-c19286380c2a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over alleged [ad auction](https://substack.com/redirect/8f026a83-246b-4a45-a106-88df45dafd72?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) manipulation.

> In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/a9244fe0-9092-4485-9062-40e368d65d07?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

##### Tracksuit x Bimma:  [Collab of the Year Report](https://substack.com/redirect/ad061e3e-3ad3-479a-be99-49ed697dc158?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is here…

For the first time, [Bimma Williams](https://substack.com/redirect/55b8e54f-01f5-427a-8648-73eabe0254ff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) partnered with Tracksuit to measure sneaker collaborations the way consumers actually experience them, with real data and real cultural insight. They [analyzed](https://substack.com/redirect/ad061e3e-3ad3-479a-be99-49ed697dc158?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the drops that moved people, shifted conversations, and built real brand energy.

And built the [COLLAB Index](https://substack.com/redirect/ad061e3e-3ad3-479a-be99-49ed697dc158?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): an evaluation tool that assesses collaborations across six dimensions: Chemistry, Originality, Legacy, Leadership, Audience Engagement, and Brand Energy.

Most brands still pick collaborators based on vibes, but vibes alone don’t sell anything. The [COLLAB Index](https://substack.com/redirect/ad061e3e-3ad3-479a-be99-49ed697dc158?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) blends two data sources into one clear score that shows what worked and why:

* Consumer Reality: Tracksuit data from a survey of 2,000+ qualified US participants.
* Cultural Reality: Evaluation from Bimma’s network of creators, journalists and strategists

> It’s the simplest way for marketers to understand, and prove, what makes a collaboration truly effective. If you’re planning a brand collab in 2026, don’t pitch it without this [Collab of the Year](https://substack.com/redirect/ad061e3e-3ad3-479a-be99-49ed697dc158?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) report.

* Generation Monologue: How the [voice note](https://substack.com/redirect/dec31e36-72a5-45a1-9205-d35425b44606?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) killed the art of conversation.

* [IAB](https://substack.com/redirect/62979265-5d45-4773-a155-3f9458b3995c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launches AI transparency and disclosure [framework](https://substack.com/redirect/413e9cf8-d9c4-4076-a6ac-cd3c9194b397?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Google IP boss: We [shouldn’t pay for AI training](https://substack.com/redirect/78bc34f1-6db8-4293-9172-fd3dd25c7b58?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on ‘freely available’ content.
	+ The EU further pushes to rein-in [Google’s ad tech monopoly](https://substack.com/redirect/5bde159d-a0ce-48a8-a79e-86f870134911?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
		- Google is [appealing](https://substack.com/redirect/fd727d30-8a5e-4f94-826e-05790c22c460?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) a judge’s search monopoly ruling.
* Ted Sarandos says critics just [misunderstand](https://substack.com/redirect/4302bb8f-fb75-4f7a-a3e3-28027d9bc730?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Netflix.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!BaXH!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6a655c94-ab41-4b83-8ee6-37cf93a7d7bb_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/349fff01-3e95-47b2-9414-eedcf78fa7d1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Ai, Japanese chimpanzee](https://substack.com/redirect/668a669b-6c2b-49dc-951b-046cba3069a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) who counted and painted, dies at 49.
* NSW government [pulled plug](https://substack.com/redirect/80e595d2-a475-4621-86ed-fb9dd7e1507a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on SXSW Sydney.
* There are [29,000 people on a waitlist](https://substack.com/redirect/d1dfa83e-63af-48a3-bb80-63540bc74138?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for beans—and it’s not for the fiber.
* Gen Z has [massively turned](https://substack.com/redirect/8939f822-8f91-47a8-a416-699732701d6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on Trump, poll finds.
	+ Trump’s net approval rating among Gen Z at negative 32 percentage points. That is a huge dip from his plus-10 net approval rating with young people in February 2025, shortly after the start of his second term.

> 🧫 New Study: Time spent on [gaming and social media](https://substack.com/redirect/8ef4c4a5-c6bf-431c-839c-98362d7c74c4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) not to blame for teen mental health issues.

> “We know families are worried, but our results do not support the idea that simply spending time on social media or gaming leads to mental health problems - the story is far more complex than that,” said lead author Dr Qiqi Cheng.

> 🍚 Report Break: 41% of U.S. teens ages 13 to 17 say they support banning middle and high school students from using cellphones during class, according to a [Pew Research Center survey](https://substack.com/redirect/8d6dc79b-bc29-41c6-8eaa-6e092fe7bdb0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) conducted in fall 2025. Still, roughly half of teens oppose such a ban. And 9% are not sure.

* ‘Sesame Street’s [largest digital library](https://substack.com/redirect/c8c3294a-3792-4f97-bc15-070aa2531e80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is now on YouTube.
* [YouTube TV](https://substack.com/redirect/fea45500-ae3f-4196-9122-7e52e99953e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) quietly expands its content for subscribers.
* YouTube creator partnerships require 90-day attribution windows, analysis [reveals](https://substack.com/redirect/541d4122-d101-4373-ac5e-6317277cfe49?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Bryson DeChambeau: Majors, YouTube-only path ‘[incredibly viable option](https://substack.com/redirect/18fdff30-9885-43c6-9883-04873302c95e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).’
* YouTube’s [latest UI tweak](https://substack.com/redirect/59c72efc-e01b-4bcd-a77f-fa51479c5304?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has commenters seeing red.
* [Kai Cenat](https://substack.com/redirect/91a3ddaf-6b45-4fca-a253-cff27b8a070c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reveals secret YouTube channel & new clothing brand amid Twitch hiatus.
* Google Health AI Overviews [cite YouTube](https://substack.com/redirect/7188a5e2-741d-43a6-ab22-7222636378ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) more than any hospital site.
	+ AI Overviews appeared on more than 82% of health-related searches in Germany. It cites figures including 55% of chatbot users trusting AI for health advice and 16% saying they’ve ignored a doctor’s advice because AI said otherwise.

* Google now [prioritising](https://substack.com/redirect/7372a088-ea51-4c12-9421-cb9c3fb603e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Youtube and X over publishers on Discover.
* Ad industry [braces for](https://substack.com/redirect/987de6d9-b07e-46f7-a078-448b987736e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Google antitrust ruling.
* [Campaign total budgets](https://substack.com/redirect/a3f674a8-52cb-4005-9006-ba4af14878bc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are now available in Search, Performance Max and Shopping campaigns.
* Google flips [OpenAI’s shopping strategy](https://substack.com/redirect/bef370a4-c6fb-4387-b43b-23734f4474be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on its head.
* Google [introduces](https://substack.com/redirect/0fe879f0-37ba-47f4-8404-9cd8dccefadc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) personalised ads into [AI shopping tools](https://substack.com/redirect/6bb3e4bb-3f01-4844-957b-71738d288551?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Google Ads [rolls out](https://substack.com/redirect/d1d46865-9849-40ad-b49a-f9c62f9d296e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) account-level placement exclusions.
* Google Ads [A/B tests](https://substack.com/redirect/9cb7cb55-70d3-4e7a-a6ad-346631d333c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) product titles & images for shopping ads.
* Google Ads [Brand Linking Experiment](https://substack.com/redirect/e773a254-aec8-4c80-b886-e87161a20d8f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) feature.
* Google Ads makes [Manual CPC](https://substack.com/redirect/bcf08bfe-d2e3-4feb-aa41-790f505c085e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) easier to find.

---

* Google brings [its AI videomaker](https://substack.com/redirect/40336cd2-d8ca-403f-858c-71eefa2c3762?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to Workspace users.
* Google’s [latest core update](https://substack.com/redirect/5fd66d6c-be5f-4ac1-ba3e-d5d5140e1c1d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) a reminder that pageviews can’t remain the primary metric.
* [DeepMind CEO](https://substack.com/redirect/31c49a29-6be6-499a-8dc1-1cd38335eb34?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is talking to Google CEO ‘every day’ as lab ramps up competition with OpenAI.
* Google [defends](https://substack.com/redirect/6f311b79-1aef-4a22-b348-9a8ff1122f7e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) AI search summaries in Rolling Stone publisher’s lawsuit.
* Google: AI Mode Checkout [can’t](https://substack.com/redirect/8a0a0198-2eaf-4d59-9126-1e1da27648b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) raise prices.
	+ ICYMI: Google announces [a new protocol](https://substack.com/redirect/f2f67c85-e803-4a82-9be7-034fc21050a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to facilitate commerce using AI agents.
* Gemini app gets dedicated [Documents history](https://substack.com/redirect/9491efdb-2c1b-4119-bf9d-2c8c3340c384?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Deep Research, Canvas.
* [Data Transmission Control](https://substack.com/redirect/8cf6afeb-77bc-4e31-aebc-ed2dbe9e7f90?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) rolls out in Google Ads.
* Google Shopping [promotion policies](https://substack.com/redirect/4bcb00dc-0997-40d3-b70f-fb2da19edc6e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are being updated to allow some more eligible promotion types.
* [Google Trends](https://substack.com/redirect/6754d75f-5eaa-49b9-93ad-4a2e838b9c84?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is redesigning its Explore page, using Gemini to find and understand trending searches.
* The Gemini app has generated [1 billion Nano Banana Pro images](https://substack.com/redirect/50151bfe-90ab-4508-a74b-291aa86f20ff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in under two months.

> 👀 Report Break: [Google](https://substack.com/redirect/c087c752-e396-4ac1-9cb3-9e2a63b12a7f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) search traffic to publishers declined globally by a third in the year to November, according to new [Chartbeat](https://substack.com/redirect/9960ea84-7732-453b-bb4e-4e8424a912a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) [data](https://substack.com/redirect/9960ea84-7732-453b-bb4e-4e8424a912a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). In addition, referrals to more than 2,500 publisher websites [from Google Discover](https://substack.com/redirect/9f3faf05-9b62-44bb-a9ad-4a37bfae4be4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), a feed served to users on Google’s native mobile apps and within its Android operating system, were down 21% year on year.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!J7DY!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Facd06a12-7508-4bfa-91b1-8c434a20fc84_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/0bd83b94-4ebd-403f-8804-78d4ad26c08b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Mother of Elon Musk’s child [sues](https://substack.com/redirect/2d3d7ab0-2dc0-4bb8-8aca-1bc5cd469fa6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) xAI over Grok abuse.
* Women’s, advocacy groups call on [Apple, Google](https://substack.com/redirect/709c4434-9917-41b2-a264-43e672b5f2e2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to drop X and Grok from app stores.
* Updated EPA rules put xAI’s ‘[Colossus](https://substack.com/redirect/b58b7620-a925-455c-9405-91adc1e56782?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ AI data center in legal limbo.
* Korea [rejects](https://substack.com/redirect/9d7e3330-90d4-4049-a5c1-a082bf2fe446?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Elon Musk’s request for documents in lawsuit against Apple.
* Elon Musk’s X bans access to ‘[InfoFi](https://substack.com/redirect/7cc8ae46-261d-4c5c-99ed-1347268d7853?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ crypto projects amid ‘AI Slop’ backlash.
* [Philippines](https://substack.com/redirect/c7e6d4fc-ba9f-4710-af10-066b42fed8b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) follows Indonesia, Malaysia to ban Grok over AI deepfakes.
	+ The [latest](https://substack.com/redirect/85fa8b3c-3087-4fe1-a118-232a496f1a48?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on Grok’s gross AI deepfakes problem.
* Senate passes [Defiance Act](https://substack.com/redirect/087c461f-8090-450f-abce-1bca2819c362?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for a second time to address Grok deepfakes.

---

* ‘[More focused on advertising than ever before’](https://substack.com/redirect/ee5bbece-29f2-44d6-ba4e-c588482f9ef7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): Nearly all of X’s top 100 advertisers returned, ads boss claims.
	+ These companies [advertised on X](https://substack.com/redirect/a2336a20-c5f2-4875-aba3-da89496fb291?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Grok produced sexualized images of kids.
* Elon Musk [promises](https://substack.com/redirect/dd536190-6164-4218-a5e7-ef5051f2ae18?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to open-source [X’s algorithm](https://substack.com/redirect/b1295023-a468-408b-8e25-4969fad4f492?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in the next few days.
* X will soon let users [attach images](https://substack.com/redirect/cfcacf36-299b-431e-8679-98bed808376a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to polls.
* X [doubles](https://substack.com/redirect/88b2a2f8-6279-4be2-8df4-cf9c62161b71?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) creator payouts and changes how payouts are calculated.
	+ X is [offering](https://substack.com/redirect/b80a341c-bee4-46c4-b179-33b1828e6963?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) [$1mn prize](https://substack.com/redirect/b80a341c-bee4-46c4-b179-33b1828e6963?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for the top performing long-form article creator.
		- The [winner](https://substack.com/redirect/a2f45b94-9a4d-4edf-bd16-4183bbd06bcc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is likely to be Dan Koe with his article reaching over 135 million views.
* X [UK revenues drop nearly 60%](https://substack.com/redirect/b63679f0-fc56-45e5-9cec-e959d8e07a82?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in a year as content concerns spook advertisers.
* [Media Matters](https://substack.com/redirect/683158df-98c7-480d-a237-ad6646c0c305?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shutters X account amid legal dispute with Elon Musk.
* X [accuses](https://substack.com/redirect/2ec76e95-06e5-4f69-a20e-0416679e4b6c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) music publishers of ‘weaponizing’ DMCA takedowns.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!85wj!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F20a7b272-bbeb-4fe3-a619-d76e41822174_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/cfdcc18b-6dec-4688-a7e9-9612681c0e9a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Pinterest [publishes](https://substack.com/redirect/cd4a9b4e-2130-48e4-acf9-7a036ec18214?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) 2026 marketing playbook.
	+ Court sides with Pinterest in [copyright case](https://substack.com/redirect/839b8db7-33af-4128-8104-1d588797649e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) brought by photographer over push notifications.
	+ Pinterest [to debut](https://substack.com/redirect/7835edc0-7153-4408-bc78-27ac09bc9c05?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shoppable connected TV series in March.
	+ Pinterest says, “[Gen Z](https://substack.com/redirect/f11d3a32-3802-4fed-b43e-78ad88701a2c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is taking back their taste.”
	+ Pinterest will release [financial results](https://substack.com/redirect/afdc6eda-4798-4f39-8b11-746769370c08?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for the fourth quarter and full year 2025 on Thursday, February 12th, 2026.
* [Roblox’s](https://substack.com/redirect/1536b219-4e0b-4821-a43a-e2b2000cbfc3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) AI-powered age verification is a complete [mess](https://substack.com/redirect/310e6e6b-9fb4-4f07-b311-106d3883c0ec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Roblox surges as [a new brainrot game](https://substack.com/redirect/4b34690e-c0b7-49bc-8a9a-8505b3e7481d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) climbs the engagement charts.
	+ Roblox’s new ads promote brands and top creators on the [homepage](https://substack.com/redirect/08cbde23-e0fa-4572-b3fd-44757e79af9b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* ‘[ELITE](https://substack.com/redirect/95edb6f1-cd52-428b-9657-21eb30d9f39f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: The Palantir App ICE uses to find neighbourhoods to raid.
* [Bluesky](https://substack.com/redirect/291db906-5fe3-4e1d-8838-4cbc3e269df4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is expanding the beta of its Live Now feature.
* [Polymarket](https://substack.com/redirect/49f94d69-0598-47dc-afe4-49ac8e1392c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) steps into legal gray area by hosting trades on war.
* [Slack](https://substack.com/redirect/6e0214bb-cce6-4dd1-866a-8e5df11bb315?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launches AI-powered Slackbot for Business+ orgs.
* [Disney+](https://substack.com/redirect/ba0ca605-22bf-4ced-9a67-6802cf238aba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) brings a massive catalog of entertainment content to Meta Quest.
* [Kroger](https://substack.com/redirect/63cd106a-e2e1-4632-9058-0304d942ccec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) kicks off online shopping with Uber nationwide.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!nHh0!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa658a05a-2645-4ce0-85ea-ab352264b4f5_1920x1080.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/13ab5622-c4cd-4b00-9b61-51a24f798b0d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Netflix preparing [all-cash offer](https://substack.com/redirect/ecda637f-5220-45d8-8ca6-53cd1bc8088f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Warner Bros to fend off Paramount.
	+ Judge [rejects](https://substack.com/redirect/f49c9bdd-743e-407c-bff0-d3b2d56e4e80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Paramount’s request to expedite case against Warner Bros.
* Instacart reveals [2026 Snacktime Report](https://substack.com/redirect/03c360a4-bf42-4ec3-bd40-745fb9da85d4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ahead of the Big Game.
* [L.A. Reported](https://substack.com/redirect/0546390b-cdcf-4f76-a86f-579383048085?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) prepares for launch as a weekly Substack that “won’t waste readers’ time.”
* Fintech firm Betterment confirms [data breach](https://substack.com/redirect/78abda2f-66b6-48db-aa7e-b847ee39b613?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after hackers send fake crypto scam notification to users.
* VSCO adds [prompt-based image editing](https://substack.com/redirect/6284f78d-de5b-4832-bd0a-7a0e3e10528d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as photographers increasingly embrace AI.
* Netflix wanted [to reinvent](https://substack.com/redirect/5c09c4e3-6370-46c1-9b4c-1ed715f0fed7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Live TV. It hasn’t been easy.
	+ Netflix viewers really are ready to rumble, watching more than [500 million hours of WWE](https://substack.com/redirect/fdb680d2-ed67-4d5e-a32c-8656b5048153?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2025.
* Craigslist founder Craig Newmark says [he’ll pull back](https://substack.com/redirect/19568e5b-b3c3-42e5-a4b9-66fc242e6507?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from funding journalism.
* India antitrust watchdog grows impatient over [Apple’s efforts](https://substack.com/redirect/623d50e0-cbb9-4f4b-96e8-c736745e65c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to delay investigation.
* Precisify deploys [$26 million investment](https://substack.com/redirect/a9e27a27-66ce-404b-b013-930d005f9b5c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to buy into the booming creator marketplace.

* [OpenAI](https://substack.com/redirect/d338238c-b8d7-4377-9558-250096bc4f01?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) expands its low-cost [ChatGPT Go](https://substack.com/redirect/edc9071c-0c93-477c-ba65-f34f78f45860?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) subscription to global users.

* Anthropic [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/86a874c4-0a65-4db2-9e24-05dd5551eb3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Cowork, a Claude Code-like for general computing.

* AI voice start-up [ElevenLabs](https://substack.com/redirect/150c71c5-d8c1-4cb7-b53f-a7de9cd707f5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in funding talks at $11bn valuation.
	+ AI Coding Startup Replit [nears funding](https://substack.com/redirect/452456b0-6c90-4058-892c-f6199401eff7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at $9 Billion valuation
* Manus partners with [Similarweb](https://substack.com/redirect/db1f472d-2b8b-4cb4-9bf7-6f1c4337d0f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for digital marketing expertise.
	+ Manus unveils [Meeting Minutes feature](https://substack.com/redirect/cc454361-3de5-43b0-baac-52ea4d586287?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for meeting summaries.
* Former Google, Apple researchers [raising $50 Million](https://substack.com/redirect/89b1ef9d-172e-4d74-93a2-263b0877b3c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for new Visual AI Startup Elorian.
* Cloudflare [acquires](https://substack.com/redirect/48cadf07-2247-4393-a51e-08906a326133?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) AI data marketplace Human Native.

* McKinsey tests [AI chatbot](https://substack.com/redirect/2b36775e-5766-4328-8727-39161ea1a9d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in early stages of graduate recruitment.

	+ Former Perplexity Ad Exec [Taz Patel](https://substack.com/redirect/80e0aee6-7828-480d-8928-3876fdc8d49d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) joins AI Video Startup Higgsfield.
* ‘[It’s AI blackface’](https://substack.com/redirect/a4dddaf2-1d22-4336-a140-73f67b7f1704?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): social media account hailed as the Aboriginal Steve Irwin is an AI character created in New Zealand.
* [Oracle](https://substack.com/redirect/ca28de1c-83f0-43bf-a5b5-0c6bbeda2fed?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) sued by bondholders over losses tied to AI buildout.
* AI journalism startup Symbolic.ai signs deal with [Rupert Murdoch’s News Corp](https://substack.com/redirect/d610202a-7e9b-4330-8496-efdf2c4849a8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* JWX acquires [Aug X Labs](https://substack.com/redirect/6276e0d3-6080-4f9c-862c-af2ce2b490d1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for AI video transformation.
* Raspberry Pi’s new [add-on board has 8GB of RAM](https://substack.com/redirect/44471630-9ece-48f7-b90c-0ec3d54a2d64?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for running gen AI models.
* Usercentrics acquires [MCP Manager](https://substack.com/redirect/5d650d57-15f9-47bb-815a-b2c577ac109c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to bring consent and trust to AI.
* A marketing startup [launched](https://substack.com/redirect/7f09216f-90f5-46cf-afe1-a82bb52e6f1e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) an unofficial “Claude Code for Marketing,” only for it to be quickly shot down by [Nikita Bier](https://substack.com/redirect/e2452249-e8f5-45bc-91f3-0c0ad0d9b6a6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and [Elon Musk](https://substack.com/redirect/6aa703f9-5932-4530-bb19-25ec29eb22ac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

> 🪑 Report Break: According to a [new survey](https://substack.com/redirect/8199e797-d602-4606-bcb0-a6b9b86dc086?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of over 280 media leaders from 51 countries conducted by the Reuters Institute for the Study of Journalism (RISJ). It found that on average, the sample of editors, CEOs, and digital executives [expect traffic to their websites to plummet by 43% over the next three years](https://substack.com/redirect/1bdfb081-2862-405c-b250-9a89c65afac5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) — which would be a disastrous blow to their commercial operations.

> There is a renewed interest in native/brand advertising (64%), perhaps because of increased advertising budgets in this area linked to short-form video.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!0cUN!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F79af7349-0432-4bf9-83ad-f76d0fd2a362_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/e2debf10-b00a-4f94-855d-bd8861fe4f73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Microsoft and Meta [kick off](https://substack.com/redirect/53198c83-1a74-4e47-a7e8-6e09bd8cd8ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) data center PR blitz.
* Microsoft adds new customer acquisition goals and deeper visibility to [PMax](https://substack.com/redirect/c036de32-f2d9-4a39-81b1-63c5d22e0b69?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* LinkedIn articles are getting [more citations](https://substack.com/redirect/b295c187-aed2-4f00-9c44-76213d10ceb5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in AI responses.
* Recruiters say job boards like [LinkedIn are dead](https://substack.com/redirect/db3f49d6-5563-4764-bdf4-74b3bc1f945d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). Here’s how to hire instead.
* Microsoft warns that [China is winning AI race](https://substack.com/redirect/ed6ffa36-3ca9-497b-bc85-9d3af3862c2a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) outside the west.
* [LinkedIn Love](https://substack.com/redirect/aa04b1ee-f762-47ce-b1b2-1481fb9ab8b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): People screening dates, stalking exes on job market app.
* Hackers are spreading [malware through LinkedIn comments](https://substack.com/redirect/713a876f-6139-4479-b5d6-1fa6e8e48d14?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) now.
* Swiss competition authority opens probe into [Microsoft licensing fees](https://substack.com/redirect/dc992b7d-9f78-4640-843d-bac39d0a104f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Microsoft vows to ‘[pay its way’](https://substack.com/redirect/87d8be81-ac67-4aef-84e3-b0891eee4aaf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as it seeks to defuse data centre backlash.
	+ Microsoft built a ‘[Community-First AI Infrastructure](https://substack.com/redirect/04fece1a-eaa3-429c-8471-56ac0ca186fa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ framework for its [data center projects](https://substack.com/redirect/9ffc2b83-1d91-4cee-81e1-991c1ab7ed89?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* BlackRock raises [$12.5bn for AI partnership](https://substack.com/redirect/e3c70b5f-4fd9-4197-aaf5-29c6be579319?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Microsoft and MGX.
* Nvidia and Microsoft back AI breakthrough for [gene therapies](https://substack.com/redirect/56a1d811-c71b-4f2d-bf86-98cad95f8acc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Microsoft is retiring its [Office Lens app](https://substack.com/redirect/f76c734b-33cf-41b8-8d59-8910f4d20031?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on iOS and Android.
* [Algolia](https://substack.com/redirect/95d264b9-cd51-4d32-b2ab-e8c8e3e1a31b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) partners with Microsoft to deliver real-time product data into Copilot, Bing Shopping, and more.
* Microsoft [shuts down](https://substack.com/redirect/ce32275b-84d8-494f-a10e-0fdc409f60b7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) library, replaces it with AI.

> 🛵 Report Break: Microsoft [outpaced](https://substack.com/redirect/bf1c5522-60cd-4d7e-832c-472d1ad53f7a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Google in paid search spend growth, with a 16% year-over-year increase in Q4, up from 12% in Q3. Meanwhile, Amazon DSP spending rose 31% year over year, driven by offsite inventory and premium placements like Prime Video ads. 

> YouTube ad spend [increased](https://substack.com/redirect/661e7f88-1ba6-4098-8497-cab999ad6a23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) 13% year over year, with impressions up 38% and CPMs down 18%. With video now accounting for 66% of Google Demand Gen spending.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!WMjD!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F605790ce-ce9b-44a1-a77e-bc12503caa70_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/f0757fe7-1097-4f78-9d8f-3285ac1fd887?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* An investor group led by I Squared Capital has dropped its pursuit of [Ströer SE & Co.'s core advertising business](https://substack.com/redirect/3aa4b9b9-7fe2-4508-a910-54df24a5628a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Advertising group Dentsu’s push to [sell global unit](https://substack.com/redirect/e8126607-dfda-46b6-bfde-48393f9856a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) close to collapse.
* After an [oversaturation](https://substack.com/redirect/895d6bf4-bef9-4b0a-b508-07a0d916eb3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of AI-generated content, creators’ authenticity and ‘messiness’ are in high demand.
* Channel 4 leads [young viewers](https://substack.com/redirect/96b8745c-979e-4f63-9f68-9a8d56b0878c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into streaming future as fastest-growing major streamer among adults.
* 90% of Brits say journalism is important to society, as editors express lack of confidence in [ad-supported publishing](https://substack.com/redirect/1326cfbb-aec8-4a37-87cc-a87e150d70a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [TV4](https://substack.com/redirect/c4120437-3c79-4903-ae8e-78938a364dd7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shuts down terrestrial broadcasts in watershed moment.
* Publicis Groupe strikes [strategic partnership](https://substack.com/redirect/ba821e19-b5e0-40e1-892c-30f45e2bfbac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with LiveRamp.
* Taiwan wants to arrest OnePlus CEO for ‘[stealing](https://substack.com/redirect/23ed2af8-0fa8-4a58-b616-22e52044db52?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ engineers.
* How [one newsroom](https://substack.com/redirect/02e61494-a268-4ad1-8fc6-0c18cfde6209?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reaches immigrant communities where they live online.
* ‘[We don’t care if you don’t use our UX anymore](https://substack.com/redirect/d9c977c1-5279-44d0-b34b-4df9fc262803?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: Yahoo recasts its DSP as a data backbone for the agentic world.
* How L’Oréal [unlearned](https://substack.com/redirect/ea72eedf-01d5-4884-8b57-76a285af72c1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) advertising and boosted creator results 55%.
* ‘[Prove the value](https://substack.com/redirect/f7d2a104-ba6d-424a-9857-9a84e2e2e9d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: ITV takes ‘soft approach’ to generative AI ads strategy.
* Amplitude acquires [Infinigrow](https://substack.com/redirect/4b911126-fd75-4c74-b54c-2c59e62b44dd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to enhance AI-driven marketing analytics.
* Rakuten Advertising removes [PayPal Honey browser extension](https://substack.com/redirect/044d1bca-9c08-4459-b020-0a3b09cd4dcc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from its network.

> 🪑 Report Break: 37% Gen-Z would watch a creator-led co-stream in regular season; 70% say they’re likely to watch their [favorite creator’s feed](https://substack.com/redirect/0d3d003e-bd2e-46fc-9466-18550141b9ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) if that creator is co-streaming a sporting event; 62% are open to fan influence on micro-moments.

> Sports Viewers are close to satisfied, but not quite there. They currently rate their enjoyment from watching sports at 3.29, but their desired enjoyment is 3.55 (-0.26). The shortfall concentrates in the audiences most needed to grow—non-fans (-0.44) and Gen Z (-0.36), signaling that the task is to improve the feel of the product before piling on more of it.

---

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;">It’s Monday? Sorry for the delay, hit peak fever last night, better now. That’s life. Thanks for your love and patience…</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dee5825b-91a5-4ce0-91ee-af8a34dc318c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard. Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.</span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money – you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6dce20ab-c394-47da-9144-6996bbc24978?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI</a><span> plans to test </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e2168f3d-fe48-4845-aa5c-02040db9a944?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">advertising</a><span> in the U.S. for ChatGPT's free and Go </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/13609d61-cf0a-4950-adcd-cd6567d7f603?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">tiers</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Digg and Reddit creators </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fc3b0ebb-aac0-41d6-96d1-9e1da0366d73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">relaunch</a><span> Digg to compete with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2ad207a9-3b45-4cfd-ba85-040e8d72e80b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Reddit</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bc9ffb18-16d2-4ef8-a39f-6c2f444d5cec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instagram</a><span> wants you to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/97e56e7d-06ab-4710-aceb-e8422dd3d79f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">personalize</a><span> your Reels algorithm for 2026.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/18317982-878b-4ea9-98bd-f9a3bb705434?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Pinterest</a><span> just released its third annual color forecast, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fe7108de-c571-4350-9ac1-e3fc1f2391ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">identifying</a><span> five shades—Perismmon, Cool Blue, Plum Noir, and Wasabi.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/38a4a96c-257d-4876-ad1a-426ed8583bf0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Apple</a><span> to replace ChatGPT with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/89a5a2c1-1fb5-44c9-9e0f-fc4ab9b51bc4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google Gemini</a><span> in $1 billion annual deal for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/740ebb64-1e88-441f-be55-2d62ceacb5ba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4149bc9c-5b05-456c-a3b7-fe05e7b5fe80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">still allowing</a><span> users </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/98ae1950-0d9a-4b72-82cb-0f4f6da48f5c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to post</a><span> sexualised images </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e4699eba-c326-45b4-8a24-7f88987210a7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">generated</a><span> by </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/25e977fd-b0b8-439b-866e-8240bd4c0e19?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Grok AI tool</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>After </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2b5aaaaf-d312-42e6-aa31-d89ab89333fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">being pillaged</a><span> by AI companies, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1b45b6e5-34aa-4f51-a957-364ab3213eac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Wikipedia</a><span> signs deal to get paid by them.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span> Spotify’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/53512c7c-943f-4d31-91ab-52fea8303af2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new CEOs</a><span> are battling algo fatigue and angry artists.</span></p></li></ul>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/28a2562e-6307-445e-bc4c-b64b5d99f690?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Atlantic</a><span>, Penske Media, Vox </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/120fb71d-6271-4a00-b861-c19286380c2a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sue Google</a><span> over alleged </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8f026a83-246b-4a45-a106-88df45dafd72?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ad auction</a><span> manipulation.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>UK PM Keir Starmer </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8725bc6f-b59c-41e7-8d42-a85923aba5c6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">hints</a><span> he could back </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/38e456ec-4f31-4b5e-8eb8-825fa32626bd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">social media ban</a><span> for under-16s.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a9244fe0-9092-4485-9062-40e368d65d07?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a></p></blockquote>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">
<span>Tracksuit x Bimma: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad061e3e-3ad3-479a-be99-49ed697dc158?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;"> Collab of the Year Report</a><span> is here…</span>
</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>For the first time, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/55b8e54f-01f5-427a-8648-73eabe0254ff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bimma Williams</a><span> partnered with Tracksuit to measure sneaker collaborations the way consumers actually experience them, with real data and real cultural insight. They </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad061e3e-3ad3-479a-be99-49ed697dc158?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">analyzed</a><span> the drops that moved people, shifted conversations, and built real brand energy.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>And built the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad061e3e-3ad3-479a-be99-49ed697dc158?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">COLLAB Index</a><span>: an evaluation tool that assesses collaborations across six dimensions: Chemistry, Originality, Legacy, Leadership, Audience Engagement, and Brand Energy.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Most brands still pick collaborators based on vibes, but vibes alone don’t sell anything. The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad061e3e-3ad3-479a-be99-49ed697dc158?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">COLLAB Index</a><span> blends two data sources into one clear score that shows what worked and why:</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Consumer Reality: Tracksuit data from a survey of 2,000+ qualified US participants.</p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Cultural Reality: Evaluation from Bimma’s network of creators, journalists and strategists</p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>It’s the simplest way for marketers to understand, and prove, what makes a collaboration truly effective. If you’re planning a brand collab in 2026, don’t pitch it without this </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad061e3e-3ad3-479a-be99-49ed697dc158?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Collab of the Year</a><span> report.</span></p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Is Sienna Rose AI? All Signs Point to ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f9f210cb-26d4-4f8d-9f73-2c24e2b7c28d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Yes</a><span>.’</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Song </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/147a8000-27fa-4cb6-adb4-ad96c45ffb02?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">banned</a><span> from Swedish charts for being AI creation.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/675b7a29-3321-4b43-8bce-61ca31f6c0e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Golden Globes’ Polymarket partnership</a><span> slammed by </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ecd44c93-98af-4629-b4fc-8b5e7b8683f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">viewers</a><span>: ‘New low for this humiliating Awards Show.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/062797d1-a153-4d05-bc38-0526c7bafe06?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tea App is back</a><span> with a new website.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Generation Monologue: How the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dec31e36-72a5-45a1-9205-d35425b44606?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">voice note</a><span> killed the art of conversation.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>How </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4814d04b-ddcf-476b-8c2d-8a2b6d9eea1b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">WhatsApp</a><span> took over the global conversation.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/62979265-5d45-4773-a155-3f9458b3995c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">IAB</a><span> launches AI transparency and disclosure </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/413e9cf8-d9c4-4076-a6ac-cd3c9194b397?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">framework</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Google IP boss: We </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/78bc34f1-6db8-4293-9172-fd3dd25c7b58?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">shouldn’t pay for AI training</a><span> on ‘freely available’ content.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The EU further pushes to rein-in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5bde159d-a0ce-48a8-a79e-86f870134911?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google’s ad tech monopoly</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fd727d30-8a5e-4f94-826e-05790c22c460?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">appealing</a><span> a judge’s search monopoly ruling.</span></p></li></ul>
</li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Ted Sarandos says critics just </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4302bb8f-fb75-4f7a-a3e3-28027d9bc730?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">misunderstand</a><span> Netflix.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Ted Sarandos says he wants to “win box office.” </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/02a1c781-4ecc-43af-a071-2dc122b3c826?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Reed Hastings</a><span> doesn’t sound so sure.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Apple’s Mac and iPad creative apps </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2a7f8717-d9bc-4a42-b78c-1edffda44d43?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">get bundled</a><span> into “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/55528e9a-e5f4-4e82-b0f8-5af55cfd3066?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Creator Studio</a><span>” subscription.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>It’s hard to justify </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b1e5ebfb-5fa7-4373-885d-05805daf7ec8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tahoe icons</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/668a669b-6c2b-49dc-951b-046cba3069a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Ai, Japanese chimpanzee </a><span>who counted and painted, dies at 49.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>NSW government </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/80e595d2-a475-4621-86ed-fb9dd7e1507a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">pulled plug</a><span> on SXSW Sydney.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>There are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d1dfa83e-63af-48a3-bb80-63540bc74138?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">29,000 people on a waitlist</a><span> for beans—and it’s not for the fiber.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Gen Z has </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8939f822-8f91-47a8-a416-699732701d6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">massively turned</a><span> on Trump, poll finds.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"> Trump’s net approval rating among Gen Z at negative 32 percentage points. That is a huge dip from his plus-10 net approval rating with young people in February 2025, shortly after the start of his second term.</p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Drinks makers left with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0523b186-0e86-431b-ac59-c2bb83e9b243?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">lake of unsold spirits</a><span> as demand drops.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Creators say </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/66fc0fa0-0b41-4418-8ff5-474766b7a4c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Spotify pays up to twice</a><span> as much as YouTube for video podcasts.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google taps</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/391ebea2-4df3-40d0-affc-9feff1e28da7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> emails and YouTube history</a><span> in push for personalised AI.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Everything is content for the ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d2d69e8f-db0d-463f-9e41-9e64dbab6c79?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">clicktatorship</a><span>’.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0c12bdc6-d8ce-4d9d-b20e-22442a23b246?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Global AI race</a><span> makes Greenland’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4a69a8dd-ea1c-4015-ab38-9e8a64965609?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">critical minerals</a><span> a tempting target.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6a0a7cdc-c266-4b9f-b511-b2e22ebda23e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Newsrooms</a><span> are taking comments seriously again.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Bari Weiss’ </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6ad07b13-88fd-4569-9487-35534777da43?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">newest slopaganda</a><span>, \*not\* “sponsored by </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1b30684b-2b8b-4dea-9b29-d9747506422c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jack Daniel’s</a><span>.”</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Behind the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/508a46f9-07e2-4873-bd89-75a6c2a0cf8d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">unraveling of Apple’s credit-card partnership</a><span> with Goldman Sachs.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Tech leaders </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e397395c-07e0-4706-aa61-f4cddf7a4b97?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">thirsty for social media fame</a><span> are hiring these LinkedIn ghostwriters.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>🩻 Yes, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c210344b-9d4e-4550-901c-7d884cfaecc3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">doctors</a><span> use </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a3fffb51-bf88-472f-b4fb-01d976931ae4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">emojis</a><span> in medical records.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2d2d46a7-6a9b-47aa-9cc4-5893224f7b2f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Discord’s IPO</a><span> will test whether its freewheeling culture can survive Wall Street.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Hershey’s to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6ed15e1a-9166-436c-8a47-8f56df624116?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">increase advertising spending</a><span> by 20% and embrace TikTok influencers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Morgan Stanley: Most </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e172c690-89ff-4950-8583-60f274778eb0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen Zers and millennials</a><span> in the US listen to about three hours of AI music a week.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
